Subject: (CY - 7545693) Signed MIL FITFILE and additional questions

Good afternoon TPP,

Please find attached the Signed MIL from FITFILE Group.

The consumer has the following questions regarding the BULK if you are able to advise?

If I’m now understanding correctly, the strategic reporting config document is targeted at practices; explaining the steps we need to undertake with each practice using SystmOne. And when it says “explain how to enable your organisation(s) for the data extract as well as explaining how to receive the extract”, the organisations = the individual practices we want to obtain data from.

We have 8 practices with patients that we want to extract data from, so we need to follow these steps with them and replicate at each site? Yes

I assumed this document detailed steps we needed to perform. But instead, its rather configuration steps we need each practice to perform and replicate for us to be able to receive the extract? This is to decide between you and the practices. You can either provide the steps for the practice to run these reports for you and provide you the data, or if the practice allow you access to their SystmOne units by means of creating you a profile (in line with yours and the practice’s own data processing agreements)then you can configure this on their behalf within their unit.

The questions that I still have are from this document listed here. Are you able to pass these on to TPP or introduce us to an appropriate contact so that we can have this conversation to clarify the below?

Consumer supplier = FITFILE? Correct

When it states we need to identify a unit that we be used as the base to download the extract from & TPP advise using a CCG/trust reporting unit. Is this aimed at the practice rather than?

We would not work via a CCG reporting unit

Can you give an example? The instructions you have been provided are the ones we give to individual SystmOne sites. Per my advice above you can work these steps to fit around your preferred process. The 8 member practices can join one SRE group, and you can choose one SystmOne unit to run this report from – you then report on all the practices within that group.

Setting up a strategic reporting organisation group = done by FITFILE or done by the practice? Done in the practice’s SystmOne unit. This can be created by a staff member at the practice or FITFILE if they give you access via a profile.

The individual organisations (practices) will need to accept the invitation into your organisation group (FITFILE’s organisation group)? The practices to be in the SRE group will receive a task to action in SystmOne which they need to accept before their site will join the group and you can then report on them.

Step 1 is to log on to the SystmOne unit from which you wish to obtain your data extract. Does this mean we need to have a logon for each practice? Or do we need to have our own SystmOne organisation app? Please refer to my first response above

Is the configuring your gateway client step aimed at practices or FITFILE? The practices

Many thanks for your help.

Caroline Holmes

Good afternoon TPP,

Please find attached the Signed MIL from FITFILE Group.

The consumer has the following questions regarding the BULK if you are able to advise?

If I’m now understanding correctly, the strategic reporting config document is targeted at practices; explaining the steps we need to undertake with each practice using SystmOne. And when it says “explain how to enable your organisation(s) for the data extract as well as explaining how to receive the extract”, the organisations = the individual practices we want to obtain data from.

We have 8 practices with patients that we want to extract data from, so we need to follow these steps with them and replicate at each site? Yes

I assumed this document detailed steps we needed to perform. But instead, its rather configuration steps we need each practice to perform and replicate for us to be able to receive the extract? This is to decide between you and the practices. You can either provide the steps for the practice to run these reports for you and provide you the data, or if the practice allow you access to their SystmOne units by means of creating you a profile (in line with yours and the practice’s own data processing agreements)then you can configure this on their behalf within their unit.

The questions that I still have are from this document listed here. Are you able to pass these on to TPP or introduce us to an appropriate contact so that we can have this conversation to clarify the below?

Consumer supplier = FITFILE? Correct

When it states we need to identify a unit that we be used as the base to download the extract from & TPP advise using a CCG/trust reporting unit. Is this aimed at the practice rather than?

We would not work via a CCG reporting unit

Can you give an example? The instructions you have been provided are the ones we give to individual SystmOne sites. Per my advice above you can work these steps to fit around your preferred process. The 8 member practices can join one SRE group, and you can choose one SystmOne unit to run this report from – you then report on all the practices within that group.

Setting up a strategic reporting organisation group = done by FITFILE or done by the practice? Done in the practice’s SystmOne unit. This can be created by a staff member at the practice or FITFILE if they give you access via a profile.

The individual organisations (practices) will need to accept the invitation into your organisation group (FITFILE’s organisation group)? The practices to be in the SRE group will receive a task to action in SystmOne which they need to accept before their site will join the group and you can then report on them.

Step 1 is to log on to the SystmOne unit from which you wish to obtain your data extract. Does this mean we need to have a logon for each practice? Or do we need to have our own SystmOne organisation app? Please refer to my first response above

Is the configuring your gateway client step aimed at practices or FITFILE? The practices

Many thanks for your help.

TPP approve FITFILE UTE

TPP approve FITFILE MIL

FITFILE given test environment to develop solution using PIP

FITFILE complete TPP SCAL

TPP grant FITFILE access to Supported test phase

TPP approve SCAL

TPP & NHSE confirm RTCN

FITFILE target practices that we wish to receive extracts from

Ideally a practice grants access to their systmone to create a profile and set up a strategic reporting organisation group

Invite all targeted practices to that SROG

Configure gateway to automate extraction using strategic reporting config document provided by TPP

FITFILE retrieve data over an N3/HSCN connection

- Does FITFILE need to create or complete a TPP DPIA or DSA?

Subject: (CY - 7545693) Signed MIL FITFILE and additional questions

Good afternoon TPP,

Please find attached the Signed MIL from FITFILE Group.

The consumer has the following questions regarding the BULK if you are able to advise?

If I’m now understanding correctly, the strategic reporting config document is targeted at practices; explaining the steps we need to undertake with each practice using SystmOne. And when it says “explain how to enable your organisation(s) for the data extract as well as explaining how to receive the extract”, the organisations = the individual practices we want to obtain data from.

We have 8 practices with patients that we want to extract data from, so we need to follow these steps with them and replicate at each site? Yes

I assumed this document detailed steps we needed to perform. But instead, its rather configuration steps we need each practice to perform and replicate for us to be able to receive the extract? This is to decide between you and the practices. You can either provide the steps for the practice to run these reports for you and provide you the data, or if the practice allow you access to their SystmOne units by means of creating you a profile (in line with yours and the practice’s own data processing agreements)then you can configure this on their behalf within their unit.

The questions that I still have are from this document listed here. Are you able to pass these on to TPP or introduce us to an appropriate contact so that we can have this conversation to clarify the below?

Consumer supplier = FITFILE? Correct

When it states we need to identify a unit that we be used as the base to download the extract from & TPP advise using a CCG/trust reporting unit. Is this aimed at the practice rather than?

We would not work via a CCG reporting unit

Can you give an example? The instructions you have been provided are the ones we give to individual SystmOne sites. Per my advice above you can work these steps to fit around your preferred process. The 8 member practices can join one SRE group, and you can choose one SystmOne unit to run this report from – you then report on all the practices within that group.

Setting up a strategic reporting organisation group = done by FITFILE or done by the practice? Done in the practice’s SystmOne unit. This can be created by a staff member at the practice or FITFILE if they give you access via a profile.

The individual organisations (practices) will need to accept the invitation into your organisation group (FITFILE’s organisation group)? The practices to be in the SRE group will receive a task to action in SystmOne which they need to accept before their site will join the group and you can then report on them.

Step 1 is to log on to the SystmOne unit from which you wish to obtain your data extract. Does this mean we need to have a logon for each practice? Or do we need to have our own SystmOne organisation app? Please refer to my first response above

Is the configuring your gateway client step aimed at practices or FITFILE? The practices

Many thanks for your help.

TPP approve FITFILE UTE

TPP approve FITFILE MIL

FITFILE given test environment to develop solution using PIP

FITFILE complete TPP SCAL

TPP grant FITFILE access to Supported test phase

TPP approve SCAL

TPP & NHSE confirm RTCN

FITFILE target practices that we wish to receive extracts from

Ideally a practice grants access to their systmone to create a profile and set up a strategic reporting organisation group

Invite all targeted practices to that SROG

Configure gateway to automate extraction using strategic reporting config document provided by TPP

FITFILE retrieve data over an N3/HSCN connection

Hi Caroline,

Thanks again for all your support with this.

We have been struggling to digest the TPP process as it seems so different to EMIS.

Charlotte has shared some further clarification with us, see attached screenshot, which seems to confirm that TPP IM1 integration still involves liaising with each site to run extracts individually on each of their SystmOne modules (or via SRE). And as such indicated that we may prefer to set up a DSA directly at each site instead of pursuing the IM1 process. This did take me back a little and is why I have questioned this process so much. It doesn’t seem very scalable if, for example, we were looking to obtain data from large numbers of TPP practices.

I wondered if you could tell me how many of the 135 suppliers on the live supplier list are interfacing with IM1 to extract information in bulk? Are we in a minority pursuing bulk extracts? Are the majority of IM1 interface mechanisms focused on read/write patient access systems?

Many thanks and have a lovely weekend!

Warmest regards,

Good afternoon TPP,

Please find attached the Signed MIL from FITFILE Group.

The consumer has the following questions regarding the BULK if you are able to advise?

If I’m now understanding correctly, the strategic reporting config document is targeted at practices; explaining the steps we need to undertake with each practice using SystmOne. And when it says “explain how to enable your organisation(s) for the data extract as well as explaining how to receive the extract”, the organisations = the individual practices we want to obtain data from.

We have 8 practices with patients that we want to extract data from, so we need to follow these steps with them and replicate at each site? Yes

I assumed this document detailed steps we needed to perform. But instead, its rather configuration steps we need each practice to perform and replicate for us to be able to receive the extract? This is to decide between you and the practices. You can either provide the steps for the practice to run these reports for you and provide you the data, or if the practice allow you access to their SystmOne units by means of creating you a profile (in line with yours and the practice’s own data processing agreements)then you can configure this on their behalf within their unit.

The questions that I still have are from this document listed here. Are you able to pass these on to TPP or introduce us to an appropriate contact so that we can have this conversation to clarify the below?

Consumer supplier = FITFILE? Correct

When it states we need to identify a unit that we be used as the base to download the extract from & TPP advise using a CCG/trust reporting unit. Is this aimed at the practice rather than?

We would not work via a CCG reporting unit

Can you give an example? The instructions you have been provided are the ones we give to individual SystmOne sites. Per my advice above you can work these steps to fit around your preferred process. The 8 member practices can join one SRE group, and you can choose one SystmOne unit to run this report from – you then report on all the practices within that group.

Setting up a strategic reporting organisation group = done by FITFILE or done by the practice? Done in the practice’s SystmOne unit. This can be created by a staff member at the practice or FITFILE if they give you access via a profile.

The individual organisations (practices) will need to accept the invitation into your organisation group (FITFILE’s organisation group)? The practices to be in the SRE group will receive a task to action in SystmOne which they need to accept before their site will join the group and you can then report on them.

Step 1 is to log on to the SystmOne unit from which you wish to obtain your data extract. Does this mean we need to have a logon for each practice? Or do we need to have our own SystmOne organisation app? Please refer to my first response above

Is the configuring your gateway client step aimed at practices or FITFILE? The practices

Many thanks for your help.

Hi Lauren,

Whilst we get to developing our product to meet to the appropriate standards, please can you confirm what next steps would be to move forward from this to live integration.

What is the process we need to complete once we feel we our product is ready?

Is there a separate form for us to complete & submit to you, to confirm our status?

I’ve previously noted the below as our next steps, but I fear it may be a little muddled, as there are 2 x test environments?

1.         FITFILE develop solution in test environment

2.         FITFILE complete TPP SCAL 

3.         TPP approve SCAL

4.         TPP Demo form completed 

5.         TPP grant FITFILE access to Supported test phase (demo access)

6.         FITFILE tests product 

7.         TPP Witness test 

8.         TPP & NHSE confirm RTCN

9.         FITFILE target practices that we wish to receive extracts from 

a. Ideally a practice grants access to their systmone to create a profile and set up a strategic reporting organisation group

b. Invite all targeted practices to that SROG

c. Configure gateway to automate extraction using strategic reporting config document provided by TPP

1.       FITFILE retrieve data over an N3/HSCN connection

Many thanks in advance,

Annabel

TPP approve FITFILE UTE

TPP approve FITFILE MIL

FITFILE given test environment to develop solution using PIP

FITFILE complete TPP SCAL

TPP grant FITFILE access to Supported test phase

TPP approve SCAL

TPP & NHSE confirm RTCN

FITFILE target practices that we wish to receive extracts from

Ideally a practice grants access to their systmone to create a profile and set up a strategic reporting organisation group

Invite all targeted practices to that SROG

Configure gateway to automate extraction using strategic reporting config document provided by TPP

FITFILE retrieve data over an N3/HSCN connection

Good afternoon TPP,

Please find attached the Signed MIL from FITFILE Group.

The consumer has the following questions regarding the BULK if you are able to advise?

If I’m now understanding correctly, the strategic reporting config document is targeted at practices; explaining the steps we need to undertake with each practice using SystmOne. And when it says “explain how to enable your organisation(s) for the data extract as well as explaining how to receive the extract”, the organisations = the individual practices we want to obtain data from.

We have 8 practices with patients that we want to extract data from, so we need to follow these steps with them and replicate at each site? Yes

I assumed this document detailed steps we needed to perform. But instead, its rather configuration steps we need each practice to perform and replicate for us to be able to receive the extract? This is to decide between you and the practices. You can either provide the steps for the practice to run these reports for you and provide you the data, or if the practice allow you access to their SystmOne units by means of creating you a profile (in line with yours and the practice’s own data processing agreements)then you can configure this on their behalf within their unit.

The questions that I still have are from this document listed here. Are you able to pass these on to TPP or introduce us to an appropriate contact so that we can have this conversation to clarify the below?

Consumer supplier = FITFILE? Correct

When it states we need to identify a unit that we be used as the base to download the extract from & TPP advise using a CCG/trust reporting unit. Is this aimed at the practice rather than?

We would not work via a CCG reporting unit

Can you give an example? The instructions you have been provided are the ones we give to individual SystmOne sites. Per my advice above you can work these steps to fit around your preferred process. The 8 member practices can join one SRE group, and you can choose one SystmOne unit to run this report from – you then report on all the practices within that group.

Setting up a strategic reporting organisation group = done by FITFILE or done by the practice? Done in the practice’s SystmOne unit. This can be created by a staff member at the practice or FITFILE if they give you access via a profile.

The individual organisations (practices) will need to accept the invitation into your organisation group (FITFILE’s organisation group)? The practices to be in the SRE group will receive a task to action in SystmOne which they need to accept before their site will join the group and you can then report on them.

Step 1 is to log on to the SystmOne unit from which you wish to obtain your data extract. Does this mean we need to have a logon for each practice? Or do we need to have our own SystmOne organisation app? Please refer to my first response above

Is the configuring your gateway client step aimed at practices or FITFILE? The practices

Many thanks for your help.

Cc: TEAM, Im1 (NHS ENGLAND - X26) [im1.team@nhs.net](mailto:im1.team@nhs.net); Annabel Flinn [annabel.flinn@fitfile.com](mailto:annabel.flinn@fitfile.com)

Subject: (CY - 7545693) Signed MIL FITFILE and additional questions

Good afternoon TPP,

Please find attached the Signed MIL from FITFILE Group.

The consumer has the following questions regarding the BULK if you are able to advise?

If I’m now understanding correctly, the strategic reporting config document is targeted at practices; explaining the steps we need to undertake with each practice using SystmOne. And when it says “explain how to enable your organisation(s) for the data extract as well as explaining how to receive the extract”, the organisations = the individual practices we want to obtain data from.

We have 8 practices with patients that we want to extract data from, so we need to follow these steps with them and replicate at each site? Yes

I assumed this document detailed steps we needed to perform. But instead, its rather configuration steps we need each practice to perform and replicate for us to be able to receive the extract? This is to decide between you and the practices. You can either provide the steps for the practice to run these reports for you and provide you the data, or if the practice allow you access to their SystmOne units by means of creating you a profile (in line with yours and the practice’s own data processing agreements)then you can configure this on their behalf within their unit.

The questions that I still have are from this document listed here. Are you able to pass these on to TPP or introduce us to an appropriate contact so that we can have this conversation to clarify the below?

Consumer supplier = FITFILE? Correct

When it states we need to identify a unit that we be used as the base to download the extract from & TPP advise using a CCG/trust reporting unit. Is this aimed at the practice rather than?

We would not work via a CCG reporting unit

Can you give an example? The instructions you have been provided are the ones we give to individual SystmOne sites. Per my advice above you can work these steps to fit around your preferred process. The 8 member practices can join one SRE group, and you can choose one SystmOne unit to run this report from – you then report on all the practices within that group.

Setting up a strategic reporting organisation group = done by FITFILE or done by the practice? Done in the practice’s SystmOne unit. This can be created by a staff member at the practice or FITFILE if they give you access via a profile.

The individual organisations (practices) will need to accept the invitation into your organisation group (FITFILE’s organisation group)? The practices to be in the SRE group will receive a task to action in SystmOne which they need to accept before their site will join the group and you can then report on them.

Step 1 is to log on to the SystmOne unit from which you wish to obtain your data extract. Does this mean we need to have a logon for each practice? Or do we need to have our own SystmOne organisation app? Please refer to my first response above

Is the configuring your gateway client step aimed at practices or FITFILE? The practices

Many thanks for your help.

Caroline Holmes

Senior Service Analyst, Primary Care Team

NHS England

Subject: (CY - 7545693) Signed MIL FITFILE and additional questions

Good afternoon TPP,

Please find attached the Signed MIL from FITFILE Group.

The consumer has the following questions regarding the BULK if you are able to advise?

If I’m now understanding correctly, the strategic reporting config document is targeted at practices; explaining the steps we need to undertake with each practice using SystmOne. And when it says “explain how to enable your organisation(s) for the data extract as well as explaining how to receive the extract”, the organisations = the individual practices we want to obtain data from.

We have 8 practices with patients that we want to extract data from, so we need to follow these steps with them and replicate at each site? Yes

I assumed this document detailed steps we needed to perform. But instead, its rather configuration steps we need each practice to perform and replicate for us to be able to receive the extract? This is to decide between you and the practices. You can either provide the steps for the practice to run these reports for you and provide you the data, or if the practice allow you access to their SystmOne units by means of creating you a profile (in line with yours and the practice’s own data processing agreements)then you can configure this on their behalf within their unit.

The questions that I still have are from this document listed here. Are you able to pass these on to TPP or introduce us to an appropriate contact so that we can have this conversation to clarify the below?

Consumer supplier = FITFILE? Correct

When it states we need to identify a unit that we be used as the base to download the extract from & TPP advise using a CCG/trust reporting unit. Is this aimed at the practice rather than?

We would not work via a CCG reporting unit

Can you give an example? The instructions you have been provided are the ones we give to individual SystmOne sites. Per my advice above you can work these steps to fit around your preferred process. The 8 member practices can join one SRE group, and you can choose one SystmOne unit to run this report from – you then report on all the practices within that group.

Setting up a strategic reporting organisation group = done by FITFILE or done by the practice? Done in the practice’s SystmOne unit. This can be created by a staff member at the practice or FITFILE if they give you access via a profile.

The individual organisations (practices) will need to accept the invitation into your organisation group (FITFILE’s organisation group)? The practices to be in the SRE group will receive a task to action in SystmOne which they need to accept before their site will join the group and you can then report on them.

Step 1 is to log on to the SystmOne unit from which you wish to obtain your data extract. Does this mean we need to have a logon for each practice? Or do we need to have our own SystmOne organisation app? Please refer to my first response above

Is the configuring your gateway client step aimed at practices or FITFILE? The practices

Many thanks for your help.

Caroline Holmes

I assumed this document detailed steps we needed to perform. But instead, its rather configuration steps we need each practice to perform and replicate for us to be able to receive the extract? This is to decide between you and the practices. You can either provide the steps for the practice to run these reports for you and provide you the data, or if the practice allow you access to their SystmOne units by means of creating you a profile (in line with yours and the practice’s own data processing agreements)then you can configure this on their behalf within their unit.

The questions that I still have are from this document listed here. Are you able to pass these on to TPP or introduce us to an appropriate contact so that we can have this conversation to clarify the below?

Consumer supplier = FITFILE? Correct

When it states we need to identify a unit that we be used as the base to download the extract from & TPP advise using a CCG/trust reporting unit. Is this aimed at the practice rather than?

We would not work via a CCG reporting unit

Can you give an example? The instructions you have been provided are the ones we give to individual SystmOne sites. Per my advice above you can work these steps to fit around your preferred process. The 8 member practices can join one SRE group, and you can choose one SystmOne unit to run this report from – you then report on all the practices within that group.

Setting up a strategic reporting organisation group = done by FITFILE or done by the practice? Done in the practice’s SystmOne unit. This can be created by a staff member at the practice or FITFILE if they give you access via a profile.

The individual organisations (practices) will need to accept the invitation into your organisation group (FITFILE’s organisation group)? The practices to be in the SRE group will receive a task to action in SystmOne which they need to accept before their site will join the group and you can then report on them.

Step 1 is to log on to the SystmOne unit from which you wish to obtain your data extract. Does this mean we need to have a logon for each practice? Or do we need to have our own SystmOne organisation app? Please refer to my first response above

Is the configuring your gateway client step aimed at practices or FITFILE? The practices

Many thanks for your help.

Petros and Annabel,

The data elements from Primary Care records we need are:

a. General demographics: NHS number, DOB, geographical area, gender, ethnicity, smoking status -

b. Concomitant corticosteroid use

c. Adverse events: Allergic reactions (including anaphylaxis/anaphylactoid/angio-oedema), Sepsis requiring hospital admission, Malignancy, Injection site reaction, Death/date of death

I used the search function in Excel to check the above and …

Missing - geographical area, ethnicity, smoking, sepsis, malignancy

Assume we can get NHS Number, DOB, cause of hospital admission, detail around injection given and any site reaction

Some key groupings from the Excel files are below:

SRManifest

Some medication/drug e.g. sensitivity, administrationdata

Clinical codes - presume diagnosis and

A/E, Hospital admission data

Assessment data and hospital discharge data

GP History and Primary care medication

SRMapping - 2000+ lines data

Have -

Referral

A/E/MIU - data

Care plans/care pathway

Allergies

Hospital prescribing,

Admin data incl appt type, billing,

Maternity care data

Social care, mental health - DoLs, sectioning, safeguarding,

GP practice, referral, health checks

SRMappingGroup - 140 lines

Key groupings of SRMapping

We should discuss the above when convenient.

1.         FITFILE develop solution in test environment

6.         FITFILE tests product 

7.         TPP Witness test 

8.         TPP & NHSE confirm RTCN

9.         FITFILE target practices that we wish to receive extracts from 

a. Ideally a practice grants access to their systmone to create a profile and set up a strategic reporting organisation group

b. Invite all targeted practices to that SROG

c. Configure gateway to automate extraction using strategic reporting config document provided by TPP

1.       FITFILE retrieve data over an N3/HSCN connection

Good afternoon TPP,

Please find attached the Signed MIL from FITFILE Group.

The consumer has the following questions regarding the BULK if you are able to advise?

If I’m now understanding correctly, the strategic reporting config document is targeted at practices; explaining the steps we need to undertake with each practice using SystmOne. And when it says “explain how to enable your organisation(s) for the data extract as well as explaining how to receive the extract”, the organisations = the individual practices we want to obtain data from.

We have 8 practices with patients that we want to extract data from, so we need to follow these steps with them and replicate at each site? Yes

I assumed this document detailed steps we needed to perform. But instead, its rather configuration steps we need each practice to perform and replicate for us to be able to receive the extract? This is to decide between you and the practices. You can either provide the steps for the practice to run these reports for you and provide you the data, or if the practice allow you access to their SystmOne units by means of creating you a profile (in line with yours and the practice’s own data processing agreements)then you can configure this on their behalf within their unit.

The questions that I still have are from this document listed here. Are you able to pass these on to TPP or introduce us to an appropriate contact so that we can have this conversation to clarify the below?

Consumer supplier = FITFILE? Correct

When it states we need to identify a unit that we be used as the base to download the extract from & TPP advise using a CCG/trust reporting unit. Is this aimed at the practice rather than?

We would not work via a CCG reporting unit

Can you give an example? The instructions you have been provided are the ones we give to individual SystmOne sites. Per my advice above you can work these steps to fit around your preferred process. The 8 member practices can join one SRE group, and you can choose one SystmOne unit to run this report from – you then report on all the practices within that group.

Setting up a strategic reporting organisation group = done by FITFILE or done by the practice? Done in the practice’s SystmOne unit. This can be created by a staff member at the practice or FITFILE if they give you access via a profile.

The individual organisations (practices) will need to accept the invitation into your organisation group (FITFILE’s organisation group)? The practices to be in the SRE group will receive a task to action in SystmOne which they need to accept before their site will join the group and you can then report on them.

Step 1 is to log on to the SystmOne unit from which you wish to obtain your data extract. Does this mean we need to have a logon for each practice? Or do we need to have our own SystmOne organisation app? Please refer to my first response above

Is the configuring your gateway client step aimed at practices or FITFILE? The practices

Many thanks for your help.

Perhaps if I outline my understanding of how to obtain tpp data below TPP can confirm if this is the path of least resistance?

TPP approve FITFILE UTE

TPP approve FITFILE MIL

FITFILE given test environment to develop solution using PIP

FITFILE complete TPP SCAL

TPP grant FITFILE access to Supported test phase

TPP approve SCAL

TPP & NHSE confirm RTCN

FITFILE target practices that we wish to receive extracts from

Ideally a practice grants access to their systmone to create a profile and set up a strategic reporting organisation group

Invite all targeted practices to that SROG

Configure gateway to automate extraction using strategic reporting config document provided by TPP

FITFILE retrieve data over an N3/HSCN connection

- Does FITFILE need to create or complete a TPP DPIA or DSA?

Subject: (CY - 7545693) Signed MIL FITFILE and additional questions

Good afternoon TPP,

Please find attached the Signed MIL from FITFILE Group.

The consumer has the following questions regarding the BULK if you are able to advise?

If I’m now understanding correctly, the strategic reporting config document is targeted at practices; explaining the steps we need to undertake with each practice using SystmOne. And when it says “explain how to enable your organisation(s) for the data extract as well as explaining how to receive the extract”, the organisations = the individual practices we want to obtain data from.

We have 8 practices with patients that we want to extract data from, so we need to follow these steps with them and replicate at each site? Yes

I assumed this document detailed steps we needed to perform. But instead, its rather configuration steps we need each practice to perform and replicate for us to be able to receive the extract? This is to decide between you and the practices. You can either provide the steps for the practice to run these reports for you and provide you the data, or if the practice allow you access to their SystmOne units by means of creating you a profile (in line with yours and the practice’s own data processing agreements)then you can configure this on their behalf within their unit.

The questions that I still have are from this document listed here. Are you able to pass these on to TPP or introduce us to an appropriate contact so that we can have this conversation to clarify the below?

Consumer supplier = FITFILE? Correct

When it states we need to identify a unit that we be used as the base to download the extract from & TPP advise using a CCG/trust reporting unit. Is this aimed at the practice rather than?

We would not work via a CCG reporting unit

Can you give an example? The instructions you have been provided are the ones we give to individual SystmOne sites. Per my advice above you can work these steps to fit around your preferred process. The 8 member practices can join one SRE group, and you can choose one SystmOne unit to run this report from – you then report on all the practices within that group.

Setting up a strategic reporting organisation group = done by FITFILE or done by the practice? Done in the practice’s SystmOne unit. This can be created by a staff member at the practice or FITFILE if they give you access via a profile.

The individual organisations (practices) will need to accept the invitation into your organisation group (FITFILE’s organisation group)? The practices to be in the SRE group will receive a task to action in SystmOne which they need to accept before their site will join the group and you can then report on them.

Step 1 is to log on to the SystmOne unit from which you wish to obtain your data extract. Does this mean we need to have a logon for each practice? Or do we need to have our own SystmOne organisation app? Please refer to my first response above

Is the configuring your gateway client step aimed at practices or FITFILE? The practices

Many thanks for your help.

Caroline Holmes

Subject: Duplicate files in EMIS IM1 extract SFTP locations

Dear EMIS IM1 extract consumer supplier,

Further to yesterday’s message it has unfortunately not been possible to delete the duplicate files from affected SFTP locations this afternoon due to system timeouts. Following platform reconfiguration our development team will be reattempting the file delete again first thing tomorrow morning. Please note that all EMIS extracts and .csv file uploads into SFTP will run tomorrow as usual.

Apologies again for any inconvenience this is causing and a further update will be circulated when we have more information.

Best wishes,

Neil

From: Neil Martin

Sent: 22 January 2025 17:10

Subject: RE: Monthly Placeholder - IM1 Supplier Forum

Dear EMIS IM1 extract consumer supplier,

We have experienced an issue today with our .csv creation process which has resulted in two sets of files being uploaded into the majority of SFTP locations. The set where file names have the date/time information truncated (first row below) are duplicates of the set where the file name has been provided in the expected format (second row below).

There is no missing data in either of the sets of files and we are recommending where possible to ignore the incorrectly named files. We are in the process of removing the duplicates from SFTP locations and hope to have this rectified by late tomorrow afternoon pending testing and deployment.

Please note that all daily delta files will continue to be processed as normal tomorrow and will be made available in your SFTP locations when ready.

We apologise for any inconvenience this may have caused where local data loading and processing routines have not been able to handle the additional set of incorrectly named files.

Best wishes,

Neil

Subject: Monthly Placeholder - IM1 Supplier Forum
When: 04 February 2025 11:00-12:00 (UTC+00:00) Dublin, Edinburgh, Lisbon, London.
Where: Microsoft Teams Meeting

STOP! Unless you are EXPECTING THIS EMAIL do NOT click on any links, or open any attachments. This may be a phishing email that could compromise our security.

Placeholder – teams link to be sent a week in advance of meeting (due to constant updates to attendees).

Hi all

This is a placeholder for the monthly IM1 supplier forum to facilitate communication between IM1, the Consuming Suppliers, and Providing Suppliers.

If you ever have anything specific you would like to see addressed in an upcoming meeting, that you feel would be relevant to the wider group, please email this to the IM1 mailbox and we will do our best to include this in the next scheduled meeting.

Attendance is optional for Consuming Suppliers, all outputs will be emailed out after the forum or can be found on our SharePoint – Meeting Documentation and Outputs ([sharepoint.com](http://sharepoint.com))

Kind regards

IM1 Team

**This is an automated response**

Thank you for emailing the TPP Integration team. Please ensure you have included the 7 digit reference number for your onboarding in your email. This is provided once your IM1/Direct integration request has been accepted for review. If this is not included, or followed up with, your email may not be responded to. The reference number can be located within the subject line of previous correspondence from TPP.

If you have not been generated a reference number your query will not be responded to.

Please find attached the specifications for all available SystmOne APIs (please note we do not process HL7, FHIR or GP Connect requests via this channel). The answers to the majority of onboarding queries can be found within these specifications. Please ensure you have referred to these fully to assist with your query.

If you are a new supplier and wish to integrate with SystmOne, please visit <[www.tpp-uk.com/resources/integration-request/](http://www.tpp-uk.com/resources/integration-request/)\><http://www.tpp-uk.com/resources/integration-request/> to submit a new Integration Request. Please note we can only process new Integration Requests submitted on this page and cannot process new requests via this email channel.

If you are already an assured Live IM1 integration partner and are experiencing an issue, this must be raised with the National Service Desk. The National Service Desk can be contacted on 0300 303 5035 / [ssd.nationalservicedesk@nhs.netmailto:ssd.nationalservicedesk@nhs.net](mailto:ssd.nationalservicedesk@nhs.net). Issues not raised via this channel will not be responded to or investigated.

Subject: Bulk EMIS - ACTION NEEDED Design test and build

Caution: This email originated from outside FITFILE. Do not click links, open attachments, share any information or respond unless you recognise the sender and know the content is safe.

Hi Annabel,

I hope you are well.

As you may be aware we issued the RTCN yesterday and EMIS have now confirmed they have begun go live steps.

With this in mind, I just wanted to make you aware of the following information regarding the switch to V8 of the BULK Schema on the 31/01/2024.

A switch over in production to a new schema will take place on Jan 31st 2024, so our consumers who are live will move to ingest the new schema on the 31/01/2023

Action needed for those in development:

For changes, please familiarise yourself with the change log on the new Schema v8.0 schema and the supporting test data that can be found here on the IM1 SharePoint site EMIS v8 schema & data

Familiarise with the ‘EMIS Extracts Services Schema v8.0’.

Impact assess the changes to your service required to allow you to ingest the updated files. Note no tables or fields are being removed from the schema. However, please be aware that validation checks may fail for example where a table field count has increased, or field headings / data types have changed.

We have also added a new table into the schema Admin_PatientHistory.

Any questions email the IM1 team.

Please could you acknowledge this email and confirm you are aware of the actions required.

Kind regards,

Caroline Holmes

Senior Service Analyst, Primary Care Team

Good morning,

We are writing to update on the latest position with the IM1 Bulk Extract, which has been paused since late June to allow for an essential review.

NHS England was contacted by several parties with concerns around information governance (IG). Specifically, the extent to which extracts are being obtained for purposes wider than those contained in the relevant Supplier Conformance Assessment Lists (SCALs). As well as the range of use cases for which data was being extracted; how it was being used; and the level of control of data being provided through the service at scale.

In response to those concerns and after careful consideration, discussion, and engagement, we took the decision to pause onboarding to IM1 Bulk Extracts for a period of 3 months to enable appropriate due diligence.

Activities during the pause

Working with legal, IG and clinical leads, we were provided with a set of recommendations, including that the IM1 Bulk Extracts process needed to be reviewed and likely uplifted.

We have subsequently conducted that review looking at how at IM1 Bulk Extracts data is utilised by suppliers and the assurance process that is undertaken.

This has resulted in the following improvements:

Uplifting the IM1 SCAL with more detailed and appropriate questions particularly to the IG, Clinical & Security tab.

Providing in the SCAL a clearer view of how IG rules are applied with the data that is obtained, which will provide more transparency to those who have a legitimate interest in the solutions. (Such as data controllers).

Greater clarity on which parts of the SCAL require completion at which stage.

Uplifting the Guidance & Supplier Information tab with roles and responsibilities for all stakeholders to reflect the Model Interface Licence.

Details of all changes are documented within the Document Control tab of the IM1 SCAL.

What happens next

There will be ongoing work that will continue following this pause to onboarding and we will work with you going forward as we delve into these elements. Some of these activities were discussed by Consuming Suppliers in the recent IM1 Collaboration and Service Improvement sessions that we held on Thursday 5th September. The feedback received in these sessions has been acknowledged and NHS England will be collating a plan to address going forward and updates will be provided in the monthly IM1 Supplier Forums and additional sessions with you as applicable.

We have attached a copy of the new IM1 SCAL (v4.6) ahead of the next Forum on Tuesday (17 September) to allow you the opportunity to review.

If there is anything in the SCAL that is unclear, or if you require further clarification, then please let us know on the call.

Post your review and any initial feedback, we will confirm the date for uplifting the IM1 Bulk onboarding pause. We are hoping to do this by 18 September as planned, dependant on your feedback.

This new SCAL will then be required to be completed by all IM1 Suppliers who are going through the onboarding process for IM1 Bulk. We will discuss with you as required, when this will apply to other integrations via IM1.

Thank you for your patience, understanding and engagement throughout this pause and we look forward to speaking to you on Tuesday.

Regards,

IM1 Team

---

Hi

Concerns have been brought to the attention of NHSE relating to the use of IM Bulk Extracts and specifically the extent to which extracts are being obtained for purposes wider than those contained in the relevant SCALs.

NHSE has determined that the IM1 Bulk Extracts process needs to be reviewed and, most likely, uplifted, and clear guidelines on appropriate use cases for these extracts should be introduced to ensure they are restricted to suitable purposes.

Consequently, NHSE will temporarily pause IM1 Bulk Extracts for new integrations or changes to existing integrations for a period of 3 months, effective immediately. This pause will allow for the proposed improvements to the current process to be implemented. Once this is complete, we will review all existing use cases and collaborate with suppliers on the next steps.

Thanks

Hi Petros,

It would depend on the interface.

A HSCN connection is not required for BULK or PFS.

A HSCN connection may be required to use the Transaction API. TPP require you to install your product at the practice and you would therefore use their HSCN connection.

Again with EMIS if you are installed at the practice you would use their connection however if you have a cloud based solution you would require a HSCN connection.

I hope this helps.

Kind regards,

HI Annabel,

I’m not sure if TPP have responded to you directly however Bridie has confirmed the following;

When an integration partner uses the Bulk API, they are required to set up the configuration for the group through a SystmOne practice. If they have the practice in mind, they would have the ability to also create the group. Therefore, TPP would not create the group for them.

I have asked Bridie to clarify regarding the dummy data within the test environment and also asked additional questions to get further clarity on your integration request and how this would work in live.

HI Annabel,

I’m not sure if TPP have responded to you directly however Bridie has confirmed the following;

When an integration partner uses the Bulk API, they are required to set up the configuration for the group through a SystmOne practice. If they have the practice in mind, they would have the ability to also create the group. Therefore, TPP would not create the group for them.

I have asked Bridie to clarify regarding the dummy data within the test environment and also asked additional questions to get further clarity on your integration request and how this would work in live.

Once I have received a response I will be in touch.

Kind regards,

Caroline Holmes

Senior Service Analyst, Primary Care Team

Hi Team,

Yesterday in the IM1 forum, I raised a query about whether TPP can support creating a strategic reporting group for a supplier like FITFILE. It was requested that I send a summary for you to raise with TPP in your meeting this week

We discussed that from previous engagement it seems like it is up to the supplier to engage with a “friendly practice” to allow them to create a SRG. However, FITFILE is currently in discussions with a national disease directory, rather than a specific primary care practice.

In the demo environment (the integration stage we are currently at with TPP) TPP had already created a SRG for FITFILE, so we wondered if this could be reproduced in the live environment?

I’m very happy to attend your call with TPP if that’s possible?

Hi Team,

Yesterday in the IM1 forum, I raised a query about whether TPP can support creating a strategic reporting group for a supplier like FITFILE. It was requested that I send a summary for you to raise with TPP in your meeting this week

We discussed that from previous engagement it seems like it is up to the supplier to engage with a “friendly practice” to allow them to create a SRG. However, FITFILE is currently in discussions with a national disease directory, rather than a specific primary care practice.

In the demo environment (the integration stage we are currently at with TPP) TPP had already created a SRG for FITFILE, so we wondered if this could be reproduced in the live environment?

I’m very happy to attend your call with TPP if that’s possible?

Best

Annabel

HI Annabel,

I’m not sure if TPP have responded to you directly however Bridie has confirmed the following;

When an integration partner uses the Bulk API, they are required to set up the configuration for the group through a SystmOne practice. If they have the practice in mind, they would have the ability to also create the group. Therefore, TPP would not create the group for them.

I have asked Bridie to clarify regarding the dummy data within the test environment and also asked additional questions to get further clarity on your integration request and how this would work in live.

Once I have received a response I will be in touch.

Kind regards,

Caroline Holmes

Subject: IM1 Supplier Forum Agenda & Outputs February 2025

Caution: This email originated from outside FITFILE. Do not click links, open attachments, share any information or respond unless you recognise the sender and know the content is safe.

Good afternoon,

Please see below the outputs for the Supplier Forum held on the 04/02/2025

Robert Marsh (Head of Service in Digital Operations and Service Management) and Justine Brightwell (Head of Delivery – Digital Primary Care) provided an update on the status relating to the IM1 onboarding process.

RM stated the focus of the Supplier Forum held (04/02/2025) was to detail the three main challenges facing current onboarding activities.

1. Challenges we are working through with our GP suppliers.

RM confirmed we are progressing the resolution of supplier challenges through ongoing open conversations with both EMIS and TPP to address the challenges Suppliers have raised. We want to reaffirm our commitment to resolving these delays as quickly as possible.

1. Capacity vs demand — specifically how number of active onboarding request is impacting our capacity.

RM explained the number of active onboarding requests in the IM1 space has been increasing. Several factors have contributed to this growth, and last year we issued a holding statement as we worked on a plan to increase our capacity. RM continued that we had hoped to share that plan with you today, outlining the roadmap to engage with all the active suppliers. Unfortunately, as you may have seen from last Thursday's announcements (30/01/25), the ongoing cuts facing NHS England have delayed our plans. We now anticipate at least four more weeks before we fully understand how these changes will affect our plans. In the meantime, we will continue working through with you to onboard as quickly as possible.

1. An update on our approach to assurance for AI connections.

RM confirmed progress has been made relating to AI assurance, and we are currently running a Proof of Concept (PoC) with a supplier. Once the assurance approach is signed off by our Clinical Stakeholders, we will begin engaging with those looking to use AI for their connections.

We're currently developing a roadmap to provide clarity on the status of IM1 approvals and aim to share it with you soon.

NHSmail update - Microsoft have announced that basic authentication will be deprecated for SMTP which may impact some of the suppliers on this call. Please see the attached document for further information.

Kind regards,

IM1 Team

Digital Operations & Service Management

Transformation Directorate

NHS England

Website: <[www.england.nhs.uk](http://www.england.nhs.uk)\>

Hi Caroline,

Of course. This is all still centred around the TPP IM1 configuration process. And whilst we received and appreciate the responses, we received from TPP, we would find it very useful to have just a short call to confirm our understanding of the process.

Perhaps if I outline my understanding of how to obtain tpp data below TPP can confirm if this is the path of least resistance?

TPP approve FITFILE UTE

TPP approve FITFILE MIL

FITFILE given test environment to develop solution using PIP

FITFILE complete TPP SCAL

TPP grant FITFILE access to Supported test phase

TPP approve SCAL

TPP & NHSE confirm RTCN

FITFILE target practices that we wish to receive extracts from

Ideally a practice grants access to their systmone to create a profile and set up a strategic reporting organisation group

Invite all targeted practices to that SROG

Configure gateway to automate extraction using strategic reporting config document provided by TPP

FITFILE retrieve data over an N3/HSCN connection

- Does FITFILE need to create or complete a TPP DPIA or DSA?

Please can we have a 20 minute meeting to confirm these are the steps to complete.

Annabel

Subject: (CY - 7545693) Signed MIL FITFILE and additional questions

Good afternoon TPP,

Please find attached the Signed MIL from FITFILE Group.

The consumer has the following questions regarding the BULK if you are able to advise?

If I’m now understanding correctly, the strategic reporting config document is targeted at practices; explaining the steps we need to undertake with each practice using SystmOne. And when it says “explain how to enable your organisation(s) for the data extract as well as explaining how to receive the extract”, the organisations = the individual practices we want to obtain data from.

We have 8 practices with patients that we want to extract data from, so we need to follow these steps with them and replicate at each site? Yes

I assumed this document detailed steps we needed to perform. But instead, its rather configuration steps we need each practice to perform and replicate for us to be able to receive the extract? This is to decide between you and the practices. You can either provide the steps for the practice to run these reports for you and provide you the data, or if the practice allow you access to their SystmOne units by means of creating you a profile (in line with yours and the practice’s own data processing agreements)then you can configure this on their behalf within their unit.

The questions that I still have are from this document listed here. Are you able to pass these on to TPP or introduce us to an appropriate contact so that we can have this conversation to clarify the below?

Consumer supplier = FITFILE? Correct

When it states we need to identify a unit that we be used as the base to download the extract from & TPP advise using a CCG/trust reporting unit. Is this aimed at the practice rather than?

We would not work via a CCG reporting unit

Can you give an example? The instructions you have been provided are the ones we give to individual SystmOne sites. Per my advice above you can work these steps to fit around your preferred process. The 8 member practices can join one SRE group, and you can choose one SystmOne unit to run this report from – you then report on all the practices within that group.

Setting up a strategic reporting organisation group = done by FITFILE or done by the practice? Done in the practice’s SystmOne unit. This can be created by a staff member at the practice or FITFILE if they give you access via a profile.

The individual organisations (practices) will need to accept the invitation into your organisation group (FITFILE’s organisation group)? The practices to be in the SRE group will receive a task to action in SystmOne which they need to accept before their site will join the group and you can then report on them.

Step 1 is to log on to the SystmOne unit from which you wish to obtain your data extract. Does this mean we need to have a logon for each practice? Or do we need to have our own SystmOne organisation app? Please refer to my first response above

Is the configuring your gateway client step aimed at practices or FITFILE? The practices

Many thanks for your help.

Caution: This email originated from outside FITFILE. Do not click links, open attachments, share any information or respond unless you recognise the sender and know the content is safe.

Hi Annabel,

For the Bulk API once Live it would be down to yourselves to liaise with sites to run the extracts in their SystmOne module. If you are doing this for a specific trust you could see if they would grant you access to their reporting unit, which would allow you to report on units under that trust from a single location however this would be at the discretion of the sites/trust. Ultimately it may be the case that you decide instead to not go through the integration process and instead set up data sharing agreements with the relevant trusts to provide you with their own data.

For the Client API, as it is desktop based, users would need to have your software downloaded on their individual machines and have SystmOne running simultaneously to connect and pull the data from at the source. Its use case is more for clinician-based softwares rather than reporting purposes. I hope this clears things up a bit for you, can you please confirm if you are still wanting to progress?

Kind regards,

Charlotte Yorston

Service Analyst

TPP

Subject: (CY - 7545693) Signed MIL FITFILE and additional questions

Good afternoon TPP,

Please find attached the Signed MIL from FITFILE Group.

The consumer has the following questions regarding the BULK if you are able to advise?

If I’m now understanding correctly, the strategic reporting config document is targeted at practices; explaining the steps we need to undertake with each practice using SystmOne. And when it says “explain how to enable your organisation(s) for the data extract as well as explaining how to receive the extract”, the organisations = the individual practices we want to obtain data from.

We have 8 practices with patients that we want to extract data from, so we need to follow these steps with them and replicate at each site? Yes

I assumed this document detailed steps we needed to perform. But instead, its rather configuration steps we need each practice to perform and replicate for us to be able to receive the extract? This is to decide between you and the practices. You can either provide the steps for the practice to run these reports for you and provide you the data, or if the practice allow you access to their SystmOne units by means of creating you a profile (in line with yours and the practice’s own data processing agreements)then you can configure this on their behalf within their unit.

The questions that I still have are from this document listed here. Are you able to pass these on to TPP or introduce us to an appropriate contact so that we can have this conversation to clarify the below?

Consumer supplier = FITFILE? Correct

When it states we need to identify a unit that we be used as the base to download the extract from & TPP advise using a CCG/trust reporting unit. Is this aimed at the practice rather than?

We would not work via a CCG reporting unit

Can you give an example? The instructions you have been provided are the ones we give to individual SystmOne sites. Per my advice above you can work these steps to fit around your preferred process. The 8 member practices can join one SRE group, and you can choose one SystmOne unit to run this report from – you then report on all the practices within that group.

Setting up a strategic reporting organisation group = done by FITFILE or done by the practice? Done in the practice’s SystmOne unit. This can be created by a staff member at the practice or FITFILE if they give you access via a profile.

The individual organisations (practices) will need to accept the invitation into your organisation group (FITFILE’s organisation group)? The practices to be in the SRE group will receive a task to action in SystmOne which they need to accept before their site will join the group and you can then report on them.

Step 1 is to log on to the SystmOne unit from which you wish to obtain your data extract. Does this mean we need to have a logon for each practice? Or do we need to have our own SystmOne organisation app? Please refer to my first response above

Is the configuring your gateway client step aimed at practices or FITFILE? The practices

Many thanks for your help.

Caroline Holmes

Subject: Review of GP data being sent via daily IM1 extracts

Caution: This email originated from outside FITFILE. Do not click links, open attachments, share any information or respond unless you recognise the sender and know the content is safe.

Dear IM1 extract consumer supplier,

We are asking all consumer suppliers to undertake a review of the GP practice data currently being received into SFTP locations through EMIS Web Data Sharing Agreements.

As a data processor EMIS always acts strictly under the instruction of GP practices as data controllers. However, delays can sometimes be experienced between third party services being contractually discontinued and the subsequent deactivation of respective Data Sharing Agreements.

Therefore, it would be greatly appreciated if you could review all data being received and provide details of any GP sites including NACS/ODS and EMIS-CDB that require deactivation to [pairing@emishealth.com](mailto:pairing@emishealth.com) and cc’ing [im1.team@nhs.net](mailto:im1.team@nhs.net).

Best wishes,

Neil

I think this finally confirms what we hoped wasn’t the case with TPP – individual contact with each TPP site for IM1 integration.

I also received an email from an Ian Rubin at XYCare, when he overheard my concerns with TPP on the IM1 live supplier forum, who offered his help. I’ve attached his overview and pricing model. I’m not sure for 8 sites his cost is worthwhile.

Please let me know what you think.

Subject: RE: (CY - 7545693) RE: (CY - 7545693) Signed MIL FITFILE and additional questions

Caution: This email originated from outside FITFILE. Do not click links, open attachments, share any information or respond unless you recognise the sender and know the content is safe.

Hi Annabel,

For the Bulk API once Live it would be down to yourselves to liaise with sites to run the extracts in their SystmOne module. If you are doing this for a specific trust you could see if they would grant you access to their reporting unit, which would allow you to report on units under that trust from a single location however this would be at the discretion of the sites/trust. Ultimately it may be the case that you decide instead to not go through the integration process and instead set up data sharing agreements with the relevant trusts to provide you with their own data.

For the Client API, as it is desktop based, users would need to have your software downloaded on their individual machines and have SystmOne running simultaneously to connect and pull the data from at the source. Its use case is more for clinician-based softwares rather than reporting purposes. I hope this clears things up a bit for you, can you please confirm if you are still wanting to progress?

Kind regards,

Charlotte Yorston

Service Analyst

TPP

Please note the dates of our upcoming configuration change freeze between 11/12/2023 – 15/01/2024, further information can be found on the SystmOne notice board.

Subject: (CY - 7545693) Signed MIL FITFILE and additional questions

Good afternoon TPP,

Please find attached the Signed MIL from FITFILE Group.

The consumer has the following questions regarding the BULK if you are able to advise?

If I’m now understanding correctly, the strategic reporting config document is targeted at practices; explaining the steps we need to undertake with each practice using SystmOne. And when it says “explain how to enable your organisation(s) for the data extract as well as explaining how to receive the extract”, the organisations = the individual practices we want to obtain data from.

We have 8 practices with patients that we want to extract data from, so we need to follow these steps with them and replicate at each site? Yes

I assumed this document detailed steps we needed to perform. But instead, its rather configuration steps we need each practice to perform and replicate for us to be able to receive the extract? This is to decide between you and the practices. You can either provide the steps for the practice to run these reports for you and provide you the data, or if the practice allow you access to their SystmOne units by means of creating you a profile (in line with yours and the practice’s own data processing agreements)then you can configure this on their behalf within their unit.

The questions that I still have are from this document listed here. Are you able to pass these on to TPP or introduce us to an appropriate contact so that we can have this conversation to clarify the below?

Consumer supplier = FITFILE? Correct

When it states we need to identify a unit that we be used as the base to download the extract from & TPP advise using a CCG/trust reporting unit. Is this aimed at the practice rather than?

We would not work via a CCG reporting unit

Can you give an example? The instructions you have been provided are the ones we give to individual SystmOne sites. Per my advice above you can work these steps to fit around your preferred process. The 8 member practices can join one SRE group, and you can choose one SystmOne unit to run this report from – you then report on all the practices within that group.

Setting up a strategic reporting organisation group = done by FITFILE or done by the practice? Done in the practice’s SystmOne unit. This can be created by a staff member at the practice or FITFILE if they give you access via a profile.

The individual organisations (practices) will need to accept the invitation into your organisation group (FITFILE’s organisation group)? The practices to be in the SRE group will receive a task to action in SystmOne which they need to accept before their site will join the group and you can then report on them.

Step 1 is to log on to the SystmOne unit from which you wish to obtain your data extract. Does this mean we need to have a logon for each practice? Or do we need to have our own SystmOne organisation app? Please refer to my first response above

Is the configuring your gateway client step aimed at practices or FITFILE? The practices

Many thanks for your help.
